[94:37.12] like data science, research engineering,
[94:39.20] statistics and business management. What
[94:41.20] makes map dolip so powerful is its
[94:43.12] flexibility. You can create all kinds of
[94:45.20] plots like line charts, bar graphs,
[94:47.12] scatter plots, histograms, pie charts

[94:51.20] you can fully customize how your plots

[94:55.20] titles and legends. When it comes to
[94:57.76] using Mattplot lip, there are two main
[94:59.52] ways to make your plots. The first one

[95:03.36] API. This is the simpler and more
[95:05.68] commonly used method. You just import it
